## External references

External papers cited by TPC are not silently copied into the paper directories. Their URLs, versions, and local applicability notes are recorded in the relevant Bridge-B files and in [REFERENCE\_PRIMEGAPS186.md](REFERENCE_PRIMEGAPS186--d3a2243b.md). A URL or citation is not treated as a locally verified full-text Markdown conversion.
